The Schedule view (`ExerciseViewActivity`\'s `ScheduleInfo`, and the schedule list rows) never showed the trainings type.\n\n## Fix\n\n- **`TrainingsTypes`**: added an `invalid` fallback member (mirroring `Intensities`) plus `fromPosition(int)` for spinner mapping, `fromName(String)` for safe XML parsing, and `displayName()` for UI output. `invalid` is appended last so the existing ordinal↔spinner-position mapping stays intact.\n- **`Schedule`**: field defaults to `invalid` instead of `null`, and the setter null-guards, eliminating the NPE on save.\n- **`XMLParser`**: enabled writing the `trainingstype` attribute (null-safe) and parsing it back via `fromName`, which tolerates old XML files that lack the attribute.\n- **`GeneralTrainingScheduleEditor`**: now reads the spinner\'s selected position and stores it on the schedule before it\'s added to the model and saved.\n- **Schedule View**: `ScheduleInfo` string gained a `Trainings Type` line rendered by `ExerciseViewActivity`, and the schedule list rows (`fragment_schedule.xml` + `MyScheduleRecyclerViewAdapter`) show the type under the name.\n\nA regression test, `trainerappmodel/src/test/.../TrainingsTypeRoundTripTest.java`, asserts that a spinner position survives write→parse and that missing/unknown values degrade to `invalid` rather than crashing.', structured_output=None, model_usage={'claude-opus-5': {'inputTokens': 58, 'outputTokens': 15576, 'cacheReadInputTokens': 1195293, 'cacheCreationInputTokens': 59933, 'webSearchRequests': 0, 'costUSD': 1.3619177499999997, 'contextWindow': 1000000, 'maxOutputTokens': 64000, 'thinkingTokens': 3125, 'canonicalModel': 'claude-opus-5', 'provider': 'firstParty', 'costBasis': 'list'}}, permission_denials=[], deferred_tool_use=None, errors=None, api_error_status=None, uuid='f73fb00f-1532-4700-95e6-185797e0e110')
